# Every forme Showdown's validateForme handles, in each validation format's
# dex, listed in a lone set (pikapro_conformance::validate::forme_cases):
# battle-only formes (Megas, Primals, Mimikyu-Busted, ...), formes an item
# or a move makes, with their required item, ability and move and without.
# `played` lines are the sets as Showdown's validator left them, which its
# battle plays. Recorded with
#   validate_fuzz --formes crates/pikapro-validate/tests/formes.txt
# (gen9vgc and championsvgc; --format all adds the custom games'
# Anything Goes).
# Never edit an expect or played line by hand.

case gen9vgc2025regi-venusaur-mega-listed
format gen9vgc2025regi
set Venusaur-Mega|Venusaurite|Thick Fat|Jolly|50|4,252,0,0,0,252|31,31,31,31,31,31|Acid Spray,Amnesia||||0|ev
expect You must bring at least 4 Pokémon (your team has 1).
expect Venusaur (Venusaur-Mega) does not exist in Gen 9.
expect Venusaur's item Venusaurite does not exist in Gen 9.
played Venusaur|Venusaurite|Overgrow|Acid Spray,Amnesia||100|Grass
end
